flask_security/decorators.py:137:    """
flask_security/decorators.py:138:    Decorator that protects enpoints through multiple mechanisms
flask_security/decorators.py:139:    Example::
flask_security/decorators.py:140:
flask_security/decorators.py:141:        @app.route('/dashboard')
flask_security/decorators.py:142:        @auth_required('token', 'session')

flask_security/decorators.py:146:    :param auth_methods: Specified mechanisms.
flask_security/decorators.py:147:    """
flask_security/decorators.py:148:    login_mechanisms = {
flask_security/decorators.py:149:        'token': lambda: _check_token(),
flask_security/decorators.py:150:        'basic': lambda: _check_http_auth(),
flask_security/decorators.py:151:        'session': lambda: current_user.is_authenticated
flask_security/decorators.py:152:    }
flask_security/decorators.py:153:
flask_security/decorators.py:154:    def wrapper(fn):
flask_security/decorators.py:155:        @wraps(fn)
flask_security/decorators.py:156:        def decorated_view(*args, **kwargs):
flask_security/decorators.py:157:            h = {}
flask_security/decorators.py:158:            mechanisms = [(method, login_mechanisms.get(method))
flask_security/decorators.py:159:                          for method in auth_methods]
flask_security/decorators.py:160:            for method, mechanism in mechanisms:
flask_security/decorators.py:161:                if mechanism and mechanism():
flask_security/decorators.py:162:                    return fn(*args, **kwargs)
flask_security/decorators.py:163:                elif method == 'basic':
flask_security/decorators.py:164:                    r = _security.default_http_auth_realm
